# Team (Dawam) area: web parity inventory

Area package: `packages/dashboard_features/team` (`dashboard_team`).
Pages: `/staff/setup`, `/staff/employees`, `/staff/attendance`, `/staff/shifts`, `/staff/team`, `/staff/approvals`,
`/staff/schedule`, `/staff/requests`, `/staff/payroll`, `/staff/reports`, `/staff/rules`.

Web reference: `/Users/shawket/Desktop/Madar/MadarDashboard`, branch `main` @ `fc2faa42` (v1.4.18). Read-only.

> **Status of this file (2026-10-08).** This is the full first-pass inventory for the area, written from a
> fresh read of every route, feature file, shared component and web test listed in each page header. The
> rows are **`TEAM-<PAGE>-NNN`** and are the canonical list: one driven test per row. An earlier
> completeness-critic pass (rows `D-001`…`D-184`, covering only Set-up, Employees, Work shifts, Attendance,
> Team, money dialogs, Requests and Approvals) is kept verbatim at the end as **Appendix Z** for traceability,
> with a D→TEAM map. Where the two disagree, the TEAM rows were re-checked against the source and win
> (one correction: the rules-first banner is mounted on Team, Schedule and Payroll, **not** Approvals).

## How to read a row

`| id | behaviour | API call | gate | i18n keys | web source |`

- **behaviour**: what the user does and sees, including validation messages, toasts, empty/loading/error words.
  English is quoted only where it helps; the words are the i18n keys in the next column.
- **API call**: `METHOD /path operationId` (operationId = the `dashboard_api` method name). "—" = no call.
  Query params in `(…)`. Every list is the Orval hook of the same name (`useListEmployees` → `listEmployees`).
- **gate**: the client check the web makes. Capability wire keys (`hr.rules.edit`). `any(a,b)` = any of;
  `everywhere(x)` = `authz.canEverywhere(x)` (the right at EVERY branch, from `/authz/me`); `ask(x)` =
  `authz.canAsk(x)` (may request approval); `owner` = `authz.owner`; `module dawam` = the org module.
  "none" = shown to whoever reaches the page (the server still decides; a refusal toasts its words).
- **web source**: `file:line` relative to `MadarDashboard/src/`; `dawam/` = `features/dawam/`,
  `staff/` = `features/staff/`, `app/` = `components/app/`, `inputs/` = `components/inputs/`.
- **Toasts**: sonner `toast.success / error / info / warning`. A failed mutation toasts
  `getErrorMessage(e)` (the server's coded refusal in the user's language, see TEAM-ALL-019) unless a row says
  otherwise. In Flutter that is `ApiException.message` plus the client-side wording overrides of TEAM-ALL-019.
- **Invalidation helpers** (`staff/util.ts:12-34`, all prefix matches on the Orval key = endpoint path):
  `invalidateStaff` = every key starting `/staff`; `invalidateEmployees` = `/staff/employees`;
  `invalidateDepartments` = `/staff/departments`; `invalidateWorkShifts` = `/staff/work-shifts`;
  `invalidateSchedules` = `/staff/schedules`; `invalidateAttendance` = `/staff/attendance` (this also covers
  `/staff/attendance/settings` and `/staff/attendance/summary`); `invalidateLeave` = `/staff/leave`;
  `invalidateRequests` = `/staff/requests` + `/staff/leave`; `invalidatePayroll` = `/staff/payroll`;
  `invalidateBranches` (`features/branches/util.ts:3`) = `/branches`.

## 3. `/staff/attendance` Attendance

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Web path | `/staff/attendance` (`routes/_app/staff/attendance.tsx` → `AttendancePage`) |
| Title | `staff.attendance` "Attendance"; nav `nav.attendance` |
| Web files read | `routes/_app/staff/attendance.tsx`, `staff/attendance-page.tsx`, `staff/attendance-dialogs.tsx`, `staff/date-time-field.tsx`, `staff/util.ts`, `dawam/period.ts`, `dawam/live.ts`, `app/export-button.tsx`, `app/stat-card.tsx` (props), `lib/excel.ts`, `lib/export-all.ts`, `lib/zoned-input.ts`, `inputs/date-field.tsx`; tests `staff/attendance-page.test.tsx`, `staff/attendance-page.methods.test.tsx`, `staff/attendance-page.zone.test.tsx`, `staff/date-time-field.test.ts` |
| Capabilities | Nav `hr.attendance.read`; NO in-page guard. Inside: `hr.attendance.create` (Add record), `hr.attendance.edit` (Correct). |
| Module | dawam |
| Realtime | None; records + summary use focus refetch. |
| API hooks | `listAttendance(from,to,branch_id?,status?,employee_id?)`, `attendanceSummary(same)`, `listEmployees()` (all statuses, picker), `listBranches(org_id)` (zones), `getAttendanceSettings()` (period start day, stale 60 s); dialogs: `listEmployees(employment_status=active)`, `listWorkShifts()`, `listBranches`, `listAttendance(from=day,to=day+40)`; mutations `createManualRecord`, `correctRecord`; export re-runs `listAttendance` with header `X-Madar-Export: 1`. |
| Invalidations | `createManualRecord` → `invalidateAttendance`; `correctRecord` → `invalidateAttendance`. |

## 4. `/staff/shifts` Work shifts

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Web path | `/staff/shifts` (`routes/_app/staff/shifts.tsx` → `WorkShiftsPage`) |
| Title | `staff.workShifts` "Work shifts"; nav `nav.workShifts` |
| Web files read | `routes/_app/staff/shifts.tsx`, `staff/work-shifts-page.tsx`, `staff/work-shift-dialog.tsx`, `staff/schedule-grid.tsx`, `staff/util.ts`, `app/list-row.tsx`, `app/section-header.tsx`, `inputs/time.ts`, `inputs/weekday-picker.tsx`; tests `staff/work-shifts-page.test.tsx` |
| Capabilities | Nav `hr.schedule.read`; NO in-page guard. Inside: `hr.schedule.create` (New shift; whole-business needs everywhere), `hr.schedule.edit` (Edit; business-wide block needs everywhere(`hr.schedule.edit`) AND (owner OR `hr.schedule.create`)), `hr.schedule.delete` (Delete; business-wide needs everywhere). The standing roster grid has NO client gate (server decides). |
| Module | dawam |
| Realtime | None; reads use focus refetch. |
| API hooks | `listWorkShifts()`, `listBranches(org_id)`, grid: `listEmployees(employment_status=active)`, `listAssignments()` (whole org); mutations `createWorkShift`, `updateWorkShift`, `deleteWorkShift`, `createAssignment`, `deleteAssignment`. |
| Invalidations | All → `invalidateStaff` (the Dawam roster lists blocks and the pattern too). The grid invalidates even when a call failed. |

## 9. `/staff/schedule` Schedule

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Web path | `/staff/schedule` (`routes/_app/staff/schedule.tsx` → `SchedulePage`); linked from Set-up "Make this week's schedule" |
| Title | `dawam.schedule` "Schedule"; nav `nav.schedule` |
| Web files read | `routes/_app/staff/schedule.tsx`, `dawam/schedule-page.tsx`, `dawam/schedule-week-bar.tsx`, `dawam/day-editor.tsx`, `dawam/schedule-checks.ts`, `dawam/coverage-editor.tsx`, `dawam/preferences-dialog.tsx`, `dawam/fairness-card.tsx`, `dawam/week.ts`, `dawam/rules-banner.tsx`, `staff/util.ts`, `staff/work-shift-dialog.tsx` (`WEEK_ORDER`); tests `dawam/schedule-page.test.tsx`, `dawam/schedule-checks.test.ts`, `dawam/week.test.ts`, `dawam/dawam-helpers.test.ts` (coverage grid) |
| Capabilities | Page `hr.schedule.read` (`<Restricted>`). Inside: `hr.schedule.edit` (edit days, open shifts, suggestions, coverage, pattern link), `hr.schedule.publish` (publish), `hr.roster.settings` (fairness card), `hr.staff.edit` (edit preferences), everywhere(`hr.rules.edit`) (decide public holidays). |
| Module | dawam |
| Realtime | None; roster / suggestions / upcoming / fairness focus-refetched. The coverage editor does NOT (editor). |
| API hooks | `listBranches(org_id)`, `roster(branch_id, from=week start, to=week end)`, `roster(branch_id, from=today, to=today+45)` (holidays), `suggestions(branch_id, week_start)` (edit right), `getCoverage(branch_id)`, `preferenceLog(employee_id)`, `fairness(month)`, `fairnessAudits()`; mutations `publish`, `postOpenShift`, `cancelOpenShift`, `decideSuggestion`, `decideHoliday`, `putDay`, `putTimes`, `moveShift`, `resetDay`, `putCoverage`, `putEmployeePreferences`. |
| Invalidations | Every mutation → `invalidateStaff` (coverage save also refetches its own `getCoverage`). |

## 10. `/staff/payroll` Payroll

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Web path | `/staff/payroll` (`routes/_app/staff/payroll.tsx` → `PayrollPage`) |
| Title | `dawam.payroll` "Payroll"; nav `nav.payroll` |
| Web files read | `routes/_app/staff/payroll.tsx`, `dawam/payroll-page.tsx`, `dawam/payroll-steps.tsx`, `dawam/payslip-print.ts`, `dawam/lines.ts`, `dawam/money-dialogs.tsx`, `dawam/rules-banner.tsx`, `staff/util.ts`, `app/page-tabs.tsx`, `app/stat-card.tsx`, `lib/excel.ts`, `lib/format.ts` (`fmtPeriod`, `fmtMoneySigned`), `hooks/use-org-modules.ts` (`useCurrentOrg`); tests `dawam/payroll-page.test.tsx`, `dawam/lines.test.ts`, `dawam/dawam-helpers.test.ts` (payslip HTML) |
| Capabilities | Page any(`hr.payroll.read`,`hr.payroll.run`) (`<Restricted>`, nothing requested). Inside: `hr.payroll.run` (approve, reopen, mark paid, decide pending pay lines), `hr.adjustments.create` (bonuses on the payslip; pay-lines tab add with either), `hr.deductions.create` (deductions, waive, override, un-waive), `hr.advances.decide` (record/review advances), `hr.expense_advances.log` (log expense), everywhere(`hr.payroll.run`) (pass the advance cap; correct a till tag). |
| Module | dawam |
| Realtime | None; all reads focus-refetched. |
| API hooks | `current()`, `listEmployees(employment_status=active)`, older month: `previewPeriod(id)` (draft) or `listPayslips(id)`; tabs: `listAdjustments()`, `listAdvances()`, `listExpenseAdvances(branch_id?)`, history sheet `listPayslips(id)`; mutations `generatePeriod`, `exportPeriodCsv`, `deleteBonus`, `deleteDeduction`, `decideAdjustment` + the money dialogs (`markPaid`, `setPeriodStatus`, `createAdjustment`, `recordAdvance`, `reviewAdvance`, `logExpenseAdvance`, `waiveDeduction`, `unwaiveDeduction`, `overrideDeduction`, `stopAdjustment`, `clearExpenseAdvance`, `reassignExpenseAdvance`). |
| Invalidations | Every mutation → `invalidateStaff`. |

---

## 13. Formatting rules and browser-side computations

Everything here is computed in the browser today; the Flutter port reproduces it exactly (pure Dart in the area
package, or a core helper where one exists). Money is integer piastres on the wire; "EGP" amounts typed by people
are pounds.

**Formatting (`lib/format.ts`, `staff/util.ts`)**
- `fmtMoney(piastres)`: currency shape of the org (EGP), 2 decimals, grouped, true minus; null/undefined → "—".
  `fmtMoneySigned` = `+EGP 20.00` / `−EGP 50.00` (payslip lines). Stat cards with `formatType="money"` use it.
- `fmtDate(iso)`: `dd MMM yyyy` in the active locale and zone (Latin digits). `fmtTime(iso, tz?)` 12-hour
  `hh:mm AM/PM` (upper-case meridiem) in `tz` or the active zone. `fmtDateTime(iso, tz?)` `dd MMM, hh:mm AM`.
  Attendance punches use the RECORD's branch timezone (`listBranches()[i].timezone`), also under "All branches".
- `fmtWireTime("HH:MM[:SS]")` → 12-hour label for wall-clock wire times (shift times, request windows, night window).
- `fmtPeriod(iso, "monthly")` → "Aug 2026" (payroll month label when a period is exactly a calendar month).
- `fmtNumber(n)` locale grouping, Latin digits (attendance distance "{{n}}m").
- `fmtMinutes(min)` (attendance cells, team "late"): `fmtElapsedMs` → "42m", "7h 25m", "1d 03h" (≥ 24 h);
  Arabic units ي/س/د with a space; negative → true minus inside the LTR isolate; null → "—".
- `fmtHours(min)` (limits, overtime totals, reports): hours + minutes, NEVER days ("56h of 48h", "7h 25m", "45m",
  "8h"); Arabic "س"/"د" with a space, wrapped LTR; negative → true minus; null → "—".
- `formatSpan(min, lang)` (shift length): "8 h", "8 h 30 min", "45 min"; Arabic "س"/"د".
- `summarizeDays(days, lang, every, none)`: Sat-first runs; a run of ≥3 → "Sat – Wed", shorter runs listed
  "Thu, Fri" (Arabic separator "، "); 7 days → "Every day"; 0 → "No days".
- `fmtLatLng` "30.04440, 31.23570" (5 decimals, LTR mono); `mapsLink` `https://www.google.com/maps?q=lat,lng`.
- Phones: canonical form (TEAM below) shown as "+digits" LTR. Employees' branch names joined with "، ".

**Dates and zones**
- `todayIso()` / `isoDaysFromToday(n)`: the active zone's calendar date (`Intl en-CA` in `getActiveTz()`), then
  UTC date arithmetic. Attendance default range = today−6 … today.
- `quickRange(kind, {periodStartDay})` (`inputs/date-field.tsx:56-84`, active zone): this/last month; this/last
  week (Saturday start); this/last pay period with start day `s` (1–28): starts the month whose day `s` is ≤ today
  (else the month before), ends the day before `s` of the next month.
- `usePeriodStartDay()` = `getAttendanceSettings().period_start_day` when 1–28, else 1.
- Week helpers (`dawam/week.ts`): `weekStartOf(iso)` = the Saturday on or before; `weekDays(start)` 7 ISO dates;
  `weekdayOf(iso)` 0=Sun…6=Sat; all as calendar dates (UTC), never through a zone. `weeksFromNow(week)` =
  round((week − weekStartOf(today)) / 7 days).
- Zoned inputs (`lib/zoned-input.ts`): `toZonedInput(iso, tz)` → "yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm" on the branch wall clock;
  `fromZonedInput(local, tz)` → UTC ISO (null for ""). Add/Correct record use the record branch's zone
  (`useBranchZone`: the branch's timezone, else the active one). Note text names the zone.
- Payroll `daysStillToCome(end, today)` = max(0, days(end − today) + 1). `monthLabel(start, end)` = "Aug 2026"
  when start is the 1st and end its month's last day, else "d1 → d2".
- `firstOpenMonth(period, today)`: no period → today's YYYY-MM; draft → the period end's YYYY-MM; else the month
  after the period end. `monthToDate("YYYY-MM")` = "YYYY-MM-01".

**Money and pay arithmetic** (integer piastres; multiply before dividing; round half away from zero)
- `readPounds(text)`: Arabic digits → Latin, "٫" → ".", strip "," and "٬", must be finite and > 0 →
  `round(n × 100)`; else null. `egpToPiastres(n) = round(n × 100)`.
- Salary calculator `rates(from, {workingDays, dayMinutes})` (`dawam/salary-calc.ts:39-52`), d100 =
  round(workingDays × 100): monthly → daily = rd(m×100, d100), hourly = rd(m×100×60, d100×mins); daily → monthly =
  rd(d×d100, 100), hourly = rd(d×60, mins); hourly → monthly = rd(h×mins×d100, 6000), daily = rd(h×mins, 60).
  `rd` = integer division rounding half away from zero. Basis: rules' `working_days_per_month` (default 26) and
  `limit_day_hours × 60` (default 480).
- `periodOf(date, startDay)` and `firstPay(monthly, hire, startDay)` = rd(monthly × daysEmployed, periodDays)
  where daysEmployed = hire → period end inclusive (e.g. 310,000 × 16/31 = 160,000).
- Late ladder (`staff/rules-preview.ts`): `selectTier(tiers, late)` = first rung with from ≤ late ≤ to (to null =
  open); late ≤ 0 → none. `tierPiastres`: piastres kind → max(0, round(value)); day_fraction →
  max(0, round(salary × value / workingDays)); minutes → max(0, round(salary × value / (workingDays × shiftMinutes)));
  zero divisors → 0. `dayPiastres(ex, days)` = round(salary × days / workingDays). Set-up example: salary
  1,200,000 pt, workingDays = rules' (or 30), shift 480 min. Rules page example: user-editable (default the same).
- Rules form (`staff/rules-form.ts`): `valuesFrom(settings, {suggest})` (suggested tiers only for an editor of an
  unsaved business with no stored tiers); `tierProblem` (sorted by from; overlap when from ≤ previous end);
  `fullBody` / `branchBody` (key-order-independent comparison; never business-only keys
  `period_start_day, advance_cap_percent, gender_mode`; inherit wins; forced keys always sent); `changedRules`;
  `defaultTierValue` 15 / 0.25 / 5000; new rung = [last end + 1 (or 1), +14], 15 minutes.
- Rules card `rulesRequest` ranges (TEAM-RUL-038); `hhmmss` adds ":00".
- Advance cap view `capView`: within = server `within_cap`, else outstanding ≤ cap when the cap is sent, else
  unknown. `salaryState`: `salary_set` false → not_set; salary null → hidden; else set.
- Payslip lines `payslipLines(slip)` (`dawam/lines.ts:90-171`): salary line amount = `base_piastres` or
  net − overtime − bonuses + deductions + advance; partial month label when 0 < paid_days < window_days; overtime
  line only when > 0; bonuses (cover/holiday labels); deductions negative, rule vs manual vs carry, waived ones
  excluded from the total; capped line = `breakdown.capped_piastres` ?? `carry_out_piastres` (positive); advance
  installments negative. Reason codes → `dawam.reason_<code>` with vars, else the server text.
- Payroll totals for an older month = sums of its rows; current month = server `totals`; paid count by hand
  excludes method "none". Rows sorted unpaid first. Pay-list export picks net > 0 by the person's pay method.
- Reports: labour share card = labour / sales × 100 to 1 decimal + "%" ("—" when sales = 0); table share =
  `labour_share_bp / 100` to 1 decimal. CSV money = piastres / 100.

**Schedule computations**
- `blocksOn(templates, weekday)` = blocks whose `valid_days` include it (missing = every day);
  `blockTimesOn(block, weekday)` = that day's own times else the default. Templates = the roster's work shifts that
  are business-wide or this branch's.
- Clash check (`dawam/schedule-checks.ts`): times → minutes; an end ≤ start runs to the next day (+1440);
  [start, end) overlap; touching ends don't clash. Used for add, edit times and move (with the colleague's day).
- Coverage grid `toGrid` (department-less needs → hour cells; band end rounds up a partial hour) and `fromGrid`
  (merge equal consecutive hours per weekday; empty/0 dropped; last band ends "23:59:59"); cell input clamps to
  integer 0–200; hours shown from min(6, earliest used) to 23.
- Roster cell maps keyed `employee|date`; `changedDays` = distinct changed cells; holidays = upcoming 45 days ∪
  the viewed week, unique by date, sorted.
- Standing grid: per employee, the first assignment seen per slot wins (server sends newest `effective_from` first).

**Attendance and team**
- `coveredBy(records, employee, date, shift?)`: a colleague's record with `covered_employee_id` = this employee,
  same date, cover pending or confirmed (and same shift when given) → their name. Rejected covers block nothing.
- `punchWindowOpen(row, now)`: state off, scheduled minutes > 0, no check-in, `punch_opens_at` ≤ now.
- Team "anyone rostered" = some row not off, or scheduled > 0, or checked in. Stat totals from summary rows.
- Add-record branch: scope branch, else the person's only branch, else the picked one of theirs.

**Validation helpers**
- `canonicalPhone(raw)` (`lib/phone.ts:24-37`): ≤ 32 chars; Arabic digits → Latin; keep digits; "00…" drop 00;
  "20…" keep; "0…" → "20…"; 10 digits starting "1" → "20…"; 10–15 digits; an Egyptian mobile (2010/2011/2012/2015)
  must be exactly 12 digits; else invalid.
- `parsePin(text)` (`dawam/geo.ts:24-58`): Arabic digits/comma/decimal normalised; URL-decoded; short links
  refused; tries `!3d…!4d…`, `?q=/query=/ll=/destination=/daddr=/center=/sll=` (optional `loc:`), `geo:`, `@lat,lng`,
  bare "lat, lng" / "lat lng"; |lat| ≤ 90, |lng| ≤ 180; rounded to 6 decimals. `inEgypt`: 21.5 ≤ lat ≤ 32 and
  24.5 ≤ lng ≤ 37.
- Import (`dawam/people.ts`): header aliases, row numbering, duplicate phone detection within the sheet, single-branch
  fallback, salary > 0 with commas stripped → piastres; CSV reader handles quotes, doubled quotes, CRLF.

**Plurals**: en.json has `_one`/`_other`; ar.json has all six CLDR forms (`_zero _one _two _few _many _other`)
for every counted key used here (`dawam.setupEmployeeCount`, `dawam.setupNoSalary`, `dawam.advanceMeta`,
`dawam.salaryMissingTitle`, `dawam.approveDaysAhead`, `dawamOps.selectAll`, `dawamOps.approveCount`,
`dawamOps.bulkTitle`, `dawamOps.bulkDone`, `dawamOps.bulkFailed`, `dawamOps.inWeeks`, `dawamOps.weeksAgo`,
`dawamOps.publishedChanged`, …). `staff.employeeCount` and `staff.changeRungs` have no plural forms (base key).

---

## 14. i18n keys missing from en.json / ar.json

**None.** A scan of every `t("…")` literal (1,016 distinct keys) and every key-like string literal (1,068) in
`features/dawam/*` and `features/staff/*` (tests excluded), plus the dynamically built keys (`staff.kind_*`,
`dawam.state_*`, `staff.att_*`, `dawam.pay_*` incl. `pay_none`, `dawam.warn_*`, `dawam.phase_*`, `dawam.adj_*`,
`dawam.via_*`, `staff.req_*`, `staff.status_*`, `dawam.flagHint_*`, `dawam.prefers_*`, `dawam.import*`,
`dawam.sg_*` from the server's `staff.sg_*`), found every key present in BOTH `src/i18n/locales/en.json` and
`ar.json` (plural keys via their `_one/_other` forms). The inline defaults in the source therefore never show;
the Flutter port uses the synced tables and needs no supplement for this area.


---

## 15. Pieces shared with other areas

**Imported by this area from other feature folders**
- `features/users/row-action.tsx` `RowAction` (admin area, `/access/users`): the icon row-action button used on
  Employees, Attendance, Work shifts, Requests, Rules (and Departments). Kit candidate.
- `features/branches/util.ts` `invalidateBranches` (admin area, `/branches`): after pinning a branch in Set-up.
- Deep link into the admin Branches page: Set-up "Add a branch" → `/branches?edit=new` (the Branches page must
  open its new-branch editor from `?edit=new`).
- Shell / dashboard_core: scope bar branch (`useScope`), org id, authz (`can`, `canAny`, `canEverywhere`, `canAsk`,
  `limitsOf`, `owner`, `ready`), module gate, org modules (`useOrgModules` for the Reports labour tab),
  `useCurrentOrg` (company name on the payslip PDF), export logo, `getErrorMessage` / `isStaleRefusal`.
- Kit (`components/app/*`, `components/inputs/*`): Page/PageHeader, DataTable, EmptyState/ErrorState, Restricted,
  ConfirmDialog, ExportButton, StatCard, StatusPill, ListCard/ListRow, SectionHeader, SegmentedControl, PageTabs,
  NumberField, DurationField, MoneyField, DateField, DateRangeField, TimeField, TimeRangeField, WeekdayPicker,
  PhoneField, EmployeePicker; `lib/excel.ts` (ExportGateway in Flutter).

**Exported by this area to other areas**
- `dawam/add-employees.tsx` `AddEmployeeDialog` with `userId` preset = "Make employee" on the admin Users page
  (`features/users/users-page.tsx:25,162`, `/access/users`). The admin area should reuse the team package's
  dialog (title "Make employee", kind fixed to Madar user, user select disabled).
- `staff/util.ts` `WEEKDAYS` used by `features/analytics/analytics-page.tsx` (weekday labels, Postgres DOW).
- `dawam/live.ts` `dawamQuery` and `dawam/refresh-button.tsx` `DawamRefreshButton` used by the reports area's
  `/reports/staff` page (`features/reports/staff/staff-discipline-page.tsx:17-18`).
- `dawam/setup.ts` `useSetupProgress` used by the shell sidebar and command palette (setup-only nav leaf,
  TEAM-ALL-003/004).
- `/staff/team` is where `/` sends a Dawam-only org (overview area / shell redirect).
- `/staff/schedule`, `/staff/team`, `/staff/rules`, `/staff/setup`, `/staff/shifts`, `/staff/employees` are link
  targets between team pages (Set-up, banners, empty states).

### Z.1 The critic file, verbatim

Area package: `packages/dashboard_features/team` (`dashboard_team`).
Pages: `/staff/setup`, `/staff/employees`, `/staff/attendance`, `/staff/shifts`, `/staff/team`,
`/staff/approvals`, `/staff/schedule`, `/staff/requests`, `/staff/payroll`, `/staff/reports`, `/staff/rules`.

Web reference: `/Users/shawket/Desktop/Madar/MadarDashboard`, branch `main` @ `fc2faa42` (v1.4.18). Read-only.

> **Critic note (2026-10-08).** The first-pass inventory for this area came back empty
> (`{"pages":[],"totalRows":0}`) and this file did not exist. Every row below was written by the
> completeness critic from a fresh read of the web source and is marked **(critic)**. No row was
> corrected because there was nothing to correct.

#### How to read a row

`| ID | Page | Action or state | Gate | What the web does (fields, validation, call, invalidation, words) |`

- **Gate**: the web capability check (`Cap.*`, any-of unless marked "everywhere" = `authz.canEverywhere`,
  the right at every branch), module, or "none" (shown to whoever reaches the page; the server decides).
- Calls are the Orval operation names (= `dashboard_api` method names). Invalidation helpers live in
  `src/features/staff/util.ts`: `invalidateStaff` = every query key under `/staff`, `invalidateEmployees`
  = `/staff/employees`, `invalidateDepartments`, `invalidateWorkShifts`, `invalidateSchedules`,
  `invalidateAttendance` = `/staff/attendance`, `invalidateLeave`, `invalidateRequests` = `/staff/requests`
  and `/staff/leave`, `invalidatePayroll` = `/staff/payroll`.
- Toasts: `toast.success/error/warning/info` (sonner). A failed mutation always toasts
  `getErrorMessage(e)` (the server's words) unless a row says otherwise.
- Strings are cited as `key` "English default". Keys under `dawam.*`, `dawamOps.*`, `staff.*`,
  `common.*`, `inputs.*`, `errors.*`.

#### Contents

| § | Page | Rows |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | Area-wide (nav, module gate, live refresh) | D-001 – D-012 |
| 1 | `/staff/setup` Set-up | D-013 – D-045 |
| 2 | `/staff/employees` Employees (+ add, import, profile, departments) | D-046 – D-103 |
| 3 | `/staff/shifts` Work shifts (+ shift dialog, standing roster grid) | D-104 – D-140 |

(Sections 4 onward follow below.)

#### 0. Area-wide

| ID | Page | Action or state | Gate | What the web does |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| D-001 (critic) | all | Sidebar group "Team" ▸ parent "Staff" (`nav.staff`, icon UserRound, basePath `/staff`) with 11 leaves in this order: Set-up, Employees, Attendance, Work shifts, Team, Approvals, Schedule, Requests, Payroll, Reports, Rules | per leaf | `src/config/nav.ts`. Labels: `nav.staffSetup` "Set-up", `nav.employees` "Employees", `nav.attendance` "Attendance", `nav.workShifts` "Work shifts", `nav.team` "Team", `nav.approvals` "Approvals", `nav.schedule` "Schedule", `nav.requests` "Requests", `nav.payroll` "Payroll", `nav.staffReports` "Reports", `nav.attendanceRules` "Rules". The parent shows only if at least one leaf is visible; it is active for any path under `/staff`. Also listed in the command palette with the same gates. |
| D-002 (critic) | all | Leaf visibility caps (any-of) | caps | setup `hrRulesEdit` (+ setup rule D-003); employees `hrStaffRead`; attendance `hrAttendanceRead`; shifts `hrScheduleRead`; team `hrAttendanceRead`; approvals any of `hrLeaveEdit, hrAttendanceEdit, hrAdvancesDecide, hrScheduleEdit, hrShiftCoverConfirm, hrOvertimeApprove, hrPayrollRun`; schedule `hrScheduleRead`; requests `hrLeaveRead`; payroll `hrPayrollRead, hrPayrollRun`; reports `hrAttendanceRead, hrPayrollRead`; rules `hrRulesEdit, hrRulesView`. |
| D-003 (critic) | all | Set-up leaf is a "setup-only" leaf | `hrRulesEdit` + incomplete | `leafVisible`: shown only while the set-up checklist is known incomplete (`useSetupProgress` ready and `count < 4`) AND the person holds `hrRulesEdit`. Disappears once all four steps are done. Test `setup-page.test.tsx` "puts the nav entry up for hr.rules.edit only while set-up is incomplete". |
| D-004 (critic) | all | Module gate: every `/staff/*` path is the Dawam module | module `dawam` | `ModuleGate` (app shell): Dawam off → EmptyState icon Blocks, `dawam.moduleOffTitle` "Not part of this business's plan", `dawam.moduleDawamOff` "Dawam by Madar is switched off for this business. Ask Madar to switch it on." Modules unknown → renders nothing; modules read failed → ErrorState `dawam.modulesLoadError` "Couldn't check what this business has switched on" + server message + Retry. Nav leaves are also hidden when the module is off. |
| D-005 (critic) | all | Pages without an in-page guard | none | Employees, Attendance, Work shifts and Requests have NO `<Restricted>`: typed by URL without the cap they render and their reads 403 into each list's error state. Set-up, Team, Approvals, Schedule, Payroll, Reports and Rules render `<Restricted>` (lock icon, `common.restrictedTitle` "Not available on this account" + the page's own `who` text, rows below). |
| D-006 (critic) | all | Refresh button in every Dawam page header | none | `DawamRefreshButton`: outline icon button (RefreshCw), tooltip + aria-label `common.refresh` "Refresh" / while busy `common.refreshing` "Refreshing…", icon spins while ANY mounted query under the page's prefixes is fetching (`aria-busy`). Click refetches the mounted (active) queries whose key starts with `/staff` (Set-up adds `/branches`), joining an in-flight fetch (`cancelRefetch:false`). Spin respects reduced motion (opacity instead). |
| D-007 (critic) | all | Live: refetch on returning to the tab | none | Dawam READS use `dawamQuery()` = `refetchOnWindowFocus: true` (stale after 30 s app-wide): coming back to the tab after ≥30 s refetches what the page shows. Editors that copy data into local state (coverage grid, dialogs, the Rules form for an editor) do NOT opt in, so a background refetch never resets typing. Flutter: refresh on app resume / route focus for reads only. |
| D-008 (critic) | all | Error state vs stale data | none | `failedEmpty(q)`: a read shows its error state only when it failed with no data; a background refetch that fails keeps the last data on screen. |
| D-009 (critic) | all | Arabic copy rule | none | Dawam/staff/errors strings in Arabic use Latin digits (test `copy-e2e-team.test.ts` "no Arabic-Indic digits in the dawam / staff / errors strings"). Numbers, phones, IBANs, IDs render LTR (`dir="ltr"`, `<bdi>`). |
| D-010 (critic) | all | Coded refusals in the user's language | none | Dawam 409/422 refusal codes have EN and AR text under `errors.*` (test "Dawam refusal codes (B-TEAM-2)": every code worded in both; the advance cap message names the figure). `getErrorMessage` turns a coded 409 into the active language. |
| D-011 (critic) | all | Week starts Saturday | none | Every Dawam week view (roster grid, schedule week, day lists) runs Sat→Fri (`WEEK_ORDER = [6,0,1,2,3,4,5]`); wire weekday values stay Postgres DOW (0 = Sunday). Day-list summaries read as ranges ("Sat – Wed", "Thu, Fri", "Every day") via `summarizeDays`. |
| D-012 (critic) | all | "Today" is the active zone's day | none | `todayIso()` / `isoDaysFromToday(n)` use the active branch/org timezone, not UTC (Cairo past midnight is still "yesterday" in UTC). `fmtHours` shows hours+minutes, never days ("56h of 48h"); Arabic units س/د, kept LTR. `fmtMinutes` "7h 25m" with a true minus sign. |

#### 6. Shared money dialogs (Team, Approvals, Payroll)

File: `src/features/dawam/money-dialogs.tsx`; tests `money-dialogs.test.tsx`, `money-dialogs.self.test.tsx`,
`expense-dialog.test.tsx`. Every dialog: Cancel (ghost) + Save; on success `invalidateStaff()` and close;
on failure toast the server's words (closed month / limit / cap), worded by situation where noted.
Amounts accept Arabic digits and separators (`readPounds`: ٩٬٠٠٠٫٥٠ = 9,000.50; must be > 0).

| ID | Page | Action or state | Gate | What the web does |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| D-137 (critic) | shared | First open month rule | – | A new pay line defaults to the first month that can take one: the open payroll period's month while it is `draft`, else the month after its end (also across a year); today's month when the caller can't read payroll (`hrPayrollRead`/`hrPayrollRun` → `current()`). The default follows the run until the month is touched. Tests M27. |
| D-138 (critic) | shared | Adjustment (bonus/deduction) dialog | caller's page gate; server limits | Title `dawam.addPayLine` "Add a bonus or deduction"; description `dawam.addPayLineHint` "Over your limit, it waits for the owner before it counts." Employee picker (active employees, NEVER the signed-in person — test "never offers the signed-in person"; hidden when the person is fixed); segmented `dawam.bonus` "Bonus" / `dawam.deduction` "Deduction"; for a bonus only, segmented `dawam.byAmount` "Amount" / `dawam.byPercent` "% of salary"; amount (`dawam.amountEgp` "Amount (EGP)" money field, or `dawam.percent` "Percent" 0 < p ≤ 100 step 0.5); reason (required `dawam.reasonRequired` "A reason is needed", ≤500, hint `dawam.reasonShown` "The employee sees this reason on their payslip."); month picker labelled `dawam.effectiveMonth` "Counts in the month of" or `dawam.recurringFrom` "Every month from" (required `dawam.monthRequired` "Pick a month", hint `dawam.monthHint` "Only open months: an approved month can't take new lines (AD-10)."); switch `dawam.everyMonth` "Every month" + `dawam.everyMonthHint` "Added to every payslip until you stop it — a meal allowance, say." `createAdjustment({employee_id, kind, reason, recurring, amount_piastres|null, percent_of_base|null, effective_date: "YYYY-MM-01"})`. Response `status:"pending"` → `toast.info` `dawam.payLinePending`; else `dawam.payLineAdded` "Pay line added". Errors worded for pay lines / month forms (REASON_REQUIRED_payLine, PERIOD_CLOSED_month). Tests AD-5, "turns a picked month into its first day". |
